Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on newtonian structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 520. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 376 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to newtonian theory, we decompose the problem into 520 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 376. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
